@@ -180,14 +180,41 @@ CREATE TABLE public.wishlist (
 -- Function to create or update a public.users entry when an auth.user is created or updated
 CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.handle_user_update()
 RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
+DECLARE
+  v_role text;
+  v_status text;
 BEGIN
-  INSERT INTO public.users (id, name, email, avatar_url, role)
-  VALUES (NEW.id, NEW.raw_user_meta_data->>'name', NEW.email, NEW.raw_user_meta_data->>'avatar_url', COALESCE(NEW.raw_user_meta_data->>'role', 'guest'))
+  -- Determine Role
+  v_role := COALESCE(NEW.raw_user_meta_data->>'role', 'guest');
+  v_role := lower(v_role);
+
+  -- Determine Status based on Role
+  IF v_role = 'host' THEN
+    v_status := 'pending';
+  ELSE
+    v_status := 'active'; -- Guests and Admins are auto-active by default (Admin created via seed/env usually)
+  END IF;
+
+  INSERT INTO public.users (id, name, email, avatar_url, role, status)
+  VALUES (
+    NEW.id, 
+    NEW.raw_user_meta_data->>'name', 
+    NEW.email, 
+    NEW.raw_user_meta_data->>'avatar_url', 
+    v_role,
+    v_status
+  )
   ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET
     name = EXCLUDED.name,
     email = EXCLUDED.email,
     avatar_url = EXCLUDED.avatar_url,
-    role = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.role, 'guest');
+    role = v_role,
+    status = CASE 
+      WHEN public.users.status = 'approved' THEN public.users.status -- Don't overwrite if already approved
+      WHEN public.users.status = 'rejected' THEN public.users.status -- Don't overwrite if rejected
+      WHEN public.users.status = 'suspended' THEN public.users.status -- Don't overwrite if suspended
+      ELSE v_status -- Update only if pending or standard state
+    END;
   RETURN NEW;
 END;
 $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

diff --git a/src/app/admin-panel/layout.tsx b/src/app/admin-panel/layout.tsx
index 6b1ce89..1db19dd 100644
--- a/src/app/admin-panel/layout.tsx
+++ b/src/app/admin-panel/layout.tsx
@@ -1,12 +1,76 @@
 import React from 'react';
 import Sidebar from './components/Sidebar';
+import { createServerClient } from '@supabase/ssr';
+import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
+import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
+
+export default async function AdminLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
+  const cookieStore = await cookies();
+
+  const supabase = createServerClient(
+    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
+    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
+    {
+      cookies: {
+        getAll() {
+          return cookieStore.getAll();
+        },
+        setAll(cookiesToSet) {
+          try {
+            cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) =>
+              cookieStore.set(name, value, options)
+            );
+          } catch {
+            // The `setAll` method was called from a Server Component.
+            // This can be ignored if you have middleware refreshing user sessions.
+          }
+        },
+      },
+    }
+  );
+
+  // Use getUser() for better security instead of getSession()
+  const {
+    data: { user },
+    error: authError,
+  } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
+
+  if (authError || !user) {
+    redirect('/auth');
+  }
+
+  // Check user role from public.users table
+  const { data: userProfile, error: profileError } = await supabase
+    .from('users')
+    .select('role')
+    .eq('id', user.id)
+    .single();
+
+  if (profileError || !userProfile) {
+    // User exists in auth but not in public.users - sign out and redirect
+    await supabase.auth.signOut();
+    redirect('/auth');
+  }
+
+  // Redirect non-admin users to their appropriate dashboards
+  if (userProfile.role === 'host') {
+    redirect('/host-dashboard');
+  }
+
+  if (userProfile.role === 'guest') {
+    redirect('/user-profile');
+  }
+
+  // Only admins can proceed
+  if (userProfile.role !== 'admin') {
+    redirect('/');
+  }
 
-export default function AdminLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
   return (
-    <div className="flex h-screen bg-background pt-[100px]">
+    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#050505] pt-[100px]">
       <Sidebar />

-        <main className="flex-1 overflow-x-hidden overflow-y-auto bg-background p-4 sm:p-6 md:p-8">
+        <main className="flex-1 overflow-x-hidden overflow-y-auto bg-[#050505] p-4 sm:p-6 md:p-8">
           {children}
         </main>
       </div>

+                      <Button
+                        variant="outline"
+                        size="sm"
+                        onClick={(e) => {
+                          e.stopPropagation();
+                          handleRowClick(property.id);
+                        }}
+                        className="text-zinc-300 border-white/10 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10 bg-transparent"
+                      >
+                        View Details
+                      </Button>
+                    )}
+                  </div>
+                </TableCell>
+              </TableRow>
+            ))
+          )}
         </TableBody>
       </Table>
     </div>
   );
+
+  const pendingProperties = properties.filter((p) => p.status === 'pending');
+  const activeProperties = properties.filter((p) => p.status === 'active');
+  const rejectedProperties = properties.filter((p) => p.status === 'rejected');
+
+  return (
+    <Tabs defaultValue="all" className="w-full">
+      <TabsList className="mb-4">
+        <TabsTrigger value="all">
+          All ({properties.length})
+        </TabsTrigger>
+        <TabsTrigger value="pending">
+          Pending ({pendingProperties.length})
+        </TabsTrigger>
+        <TabsTrigger value="active">
+          Active ({activeProperties.length})
+        </TabsTrigger>
+        <TabsTrigger value="rejected">
+          Rejected ({rejectedProperties.length})
+        </TabsTrigger>
+      </TabsList>
+
+      <TabsContent value="all">
+        {renderPropertyTable(properties)}
+      </TabsContent>
+
+      <TabsContent value="pending">
+        {renderPropertyTable(pendingProperties)}
+      </TabsContent>
+
+      <TabsContent value="active">
+        {renderPropertyTable(activeProperties)}
+      </TabsContent>
+
+      <TabsContent value="rejected">
+        {renderPropertyTable(rejectedProperties)}
+      </TabsContent>
+    </Tabs>
+  );
 };
 
 export default PropertyList;

+  // Real-time subscription for property updates
+  useEffect(() => {
+    const channel = supabase
+      .channel('host-properties-realtime')
+      .on(
+        'postgres_changes',
+        {
+          event: '*',
+          schema: 'public',
+          table: 'properties',
+          filter: `host_id=eq.${hostData.id}`,
+        },
+        (payload) => {
+          if (payload.eventType === 'INSERT') {
+            setProperties((prev) => [...prev, payload.new as Property]);
+          } else if (payload.eventType === 'UPDATE') {
+            setProperties((prev) =>
+              prev.map((p) => (p.id === payload.new.id ? (payload.new as Property) : p))
+            );
+          } else if (payload.eventType === 'DELETE') {
+            setProperties((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.id !== (payload.old as Property).id));
+          }
+        }
+      )
+      .subscribe();
+
+    return () => {
+      supabase.removeChannel(channel);
+    };
+  }, [hostData.id, supabase]);

+      </motion.header>
+

+      <AnimatePresence>
+        {isMobileMenuOpen && (
+          <motion.div
+            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: '-100%' }}
+            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
+            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: '-100%' }}
+            transition={{ type: "spring", bounce: 0, duration: 0.4 }}
+            className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] bg-[#050505] lg:hidden flex flex-col items-center justify-center pt-20"
+          >
+            {/* Background Elements */}
+            <div className="absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden pointer-events-none">
+              <div className="absolute top-1/4 left-1/4 w-64 h-64 bg-[#0ea5e9]/10 rounded-full blur-[100px]" />
+              <div className="absolute bottom-1/4 right-1/4 w-64 h-64 bg-[#0ea5e9]/5 rounded-full blur-[100px]" />
+            </div>

+                  initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}

+                  transition={{ delay: 0.1 + (i * 0.05) }}

+                    className="block text-2xl font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-white hover:text-[#0ea5e9] transition-colors w-full py-2"
+                  >
+                    {link.name}
+                  </Link>
+                </motion.div>
+              ))}
+
+              <motion.div
+                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
+                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
+                transition={{ delay: 0.5 }}
+                className="mt-8"
+              >
+                {!user ? (
+                  <Link href="/auth">
+                    <button className="relative px-12 py-4 border border-[#0ea5e9] bg-[#0ea5e9]/10 text-[#0ea5e9] font-black uppercase tracking-widest hover:bg-[#0ea5e9] hover:text-white transition-all duration-300">
+                      Sign In
+                    </button>
+                  </Link>
+                ) : (
+                  <button
+                    onClick={handleLogout}
+                    className="relative px-12 py-4 border border-[#0ea5e9] bg-[#0ea5e9]/10 text-[#0ea5e9] font-black uppercase tracking-widest hover:bg-[#0ea5e9] hover:text-white transition-all duration-300"
+                  >
+                    Sign Out
+                  </button>
+                )}
+              </motion.div>
+            </nav>
+          </motion.div>
+        )}
+      </AnimatePresence>
+    </>
   );
 };

+/**
+ * Rejects a host with a reason in the database using service_role privileges.
+ */
+// Rejects a host with a reason in the database using service_role privileges.
+export async function rejectHostWithReason(userId: string, reason: string) {
+  const supabase = await createAdminSupabaseClient();
+
+  // 1. Try to save reason to Auth Metadata (Safe fallback)
+  const { error: metaError } = await supabase.auth.admin.updateUserById(
+    userId,
+    { user_metadata: { rejection_reason: reason } }
+  );
+  if (metaError) {
+    console.warn('Failed to update rejection reason in metadata:', metaError);
+  }
+
+  // 2. Try updating public profile WITH column
+  const { data, error } = await supabase
+    .from('users')
+    .update({ status: 'rejected', rejection_reason: reason })
+    .eq('id', userId);
+
+  if (error) {
+    // Check if error is due to missing column (Postgres code 42703 usually, but message check is safer here)
+    if (error.message?.includes('column') && error.message?.includes('rejection_reason')) {
+      console.warn('rejection_reason column missing, falling back to status-only update.');
+
+      // 3. Fallback: Update ONLY status
+      const { data: fallbackData, error: fallbackError } = await supabase
+        .from('users')
+        .update({ status: 'rejected' })
+        .eq('id', userId);
+
+      if (fallbackError) {
+        console.error('Error rejecting host (fallback status-only):', fallbackError);
+        return { success: false, error: fallbackError.message };
+      }
+      return { success: true, data: fallbackData };
     }
-    await logActivity('host_rejected', `Host ${userId} has been rejected.`, userId);
-    return { success: true, data };
+
+    console.error('Error rejecting host with reason as admin:', error);
+    return { success: false, error: error.message };
   }
 
+  await logActivity('host_approval', `Host ${userId} has been rejected. Reason: ${reason}`, userId, 'Host Rejected', 'error');
+  return { success: true, data };
+}

+/**
+ * Fetches all users from public.users, but first ensures that any user in auth.users
+ * also exists in public.users. This "heals" any missing trigger executions.
+ */
+export async function syncAndFetchUsers() {
+  const supabase = await createAdminSupabaseClient();
+
+  // 1. Fetch all Auth Users (Source of Truth for existence)
+  const { data: { users: authUsers }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.admin.listUsers();
+
+  if (authError) {
+    console.error('Error fetching auth users:', authError);
+    // Fallback to just returning public users if auth fetch fails
+    const { data: publicUsers } = await supabase.from('users').select('*');
+    return publicUsers || [];
+  }
+
+  // 2. Fetch all Public Users
+  const { data: publicUsers, error: publicError } = await supabase.from('users').select('*');
+
+  if (publicError) {
+    console.error('Error fetching public users:', publicError);
+    return [];
+  }
+
+  // 3. Find users in Auth but missing in Public
+  const missingUsers = authUsers.filter(au => !publicUsers?.some(pu => pu.id === au.id));
+
+  if (missingUsers.length > 0) {
+    console.log(`Found ${missingUsers.length} users missing in public.users. Syncing...`);
+
+    // 4. Create profiles for missing users
+    for (const user of missingUsers) {
+      const role = user.user_metadata?.role || 'guest';
+      const name = user.user_metadata?.name || user.email?.split('@')[0] || 'User';
+      // Determine correct status
+      let status = 'active'; // Default for guest
+      if (role === 'host') status = 'pending'; // Host needs approval
+
+      // Use upsert to handle race conditions where the user might have been created by a trigger
+      // right after we checked.
+      const { error: insertError } = await supabase.from('users').upsert({
+        id: user.id,
+        email: user.email,
+        name: name,
+        role: role,
+        status: status,
+        avatar_url: user.user_metadata?.avatar_url || null
+      }, { onConflict: 'id' });
+
+      if (insertError) {
+        console.error(`Failed to sync user ${user.id}:`, JSON.stringify(insertError, null, 2));
+      } else {
+        console.log(`Synced user ${user.id} (${role})`);
+      }
+    }
+
+    // 5. Refetch public users to get the updated list
+    const { data: updatedPublicUsers } = await supabase.from('users').select('*');
+
+    // Enrich with metadata for rejection_reason (fallback)
+    const enrichedUsers = (updatedPublicUsers || []).map(user => {
+      const authUser = authUsers.find(au => au.id === user.id);
+      return {
+        ...user,
+        rejection_reason: user.rejection_reason || authUser?.user_metadata?.rejection_reason
+      };
+    });
+
+    return enrichedUsers;
+  }
+
+  // Enrich with metadata for rejection_reason (fallback) even if no sync details needed
+  const enrichedPublicUsers = (publicUsers || []).map(user => {
+    const authUser = authUsers.find(au => au.id === user.id);
+    return {
+      ...user,
+      rejection_reason: user.rejection_reason || authUser?.user_metadata?.rejection_reason
+    };
+  });
+
+  return enrichedPublicUsers;
+}
+

+export async function signInWithMobile(prevState: string | undefined, formData: FormData) {
+  const phone = formData.get('phone') as string;
+
+  if (!phone) {
+    return 'Phone number is required.';
+  }
+
+  const supabase = await createSupabaseServerClient();
+  const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithOtp({
+    phone,
+  });
+
+  if (error) {
+    console.error('Mobile Sign In Error:', error.message);
+    return error.message;
   }
 
-  return 'Login failed.';
+  return { success: true, message: 'OTP sent successfully' };
+}
+
+export async function verifyMobileOtp(prevState: string | undefined, formData: FormData) {
+  const phone = formData.get('phone') as string;
+  const token = formData.get('otp') as string;
+  const name = formData.get('name') as string; // Optional context for new users
+  const role = (formData.get('role') as string)?.toLowerCase() || 'guest'; // Default to guest
+
+  // Payment Details
+  const razorpayPaymentId = formData.get('razorpay_payment_id') as string;
+  const razorpayOrderId = formData.get('razorpay_order_id') as string;
+  const razorpaySignature = formData.get('razorpay_signature') as string;
+
+  if (!phone || !token) {
+    return 'Phone number and OTP are required.';
+  }
+
+  // Payment Verification for Host
+  if (role === 'host') {
+    if (!razorpayPaymentId || !razorpayOrderId || !razorpaySignature) {
+      return 'Payment verification failed: Details missing.';
+    }
+    const isPaymentValid = await verifyPaymentSignature(razorpayOrderId, razorpayPaymentId, razorpaySignature);
+    if (!isPaymentValid) {
+      return 'Payment verification failed: Invalid signature.';
+    }
+  }
+
+  const supabase = await createSupabaseServerClient();
+  const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.verifyOtp({
+    phone,
+    token,
+    type: 'sms',
+  });
+
+  if (error) {
+    console.error('OTP Verification Error:', error.message);
+    return 'Invalid OTP code.';
+  }
+
+  if (data.user) {
+    const userId = data.user.id;
+    const supabaseAdmin = await createSupabaseAdminClient();
+
+    // Check if user profile exists
+    // We MUST fetch the role from public.users to be secure
+    let { data: userProfile, error: profileError } = await supabaseAdmin
+      .from('users')
+      .select('role, status')
+      .eq('id', userId)
+      .single();
+
+    if (profileError || !userProfile) {
+      // Create new profile for mobile user
+      console.log('Mobile user profile not found, creating...');
+
+      const userRole = role === 'host' ? 'host' : 'guest';
+      const userStatus = userRole === 'host' ? 'active' : 'active'; // Hosts are auto-approved now
+      const userName = name || 'Mobile User';
+
+      const { data: newProfile, error: createError } = await supabaseAdmin
+        .from('users')
+        .insert({
+          id: userId,
+          name: userName,
+          phone: phone,
+          // Email might be null for phone users, handle schema constraints if any
+          role: userRole,
+          status: userStatus,
+        })
+        .select('role, status')
+        .single();
+
+      if (createError) {
+        console.error('Failed to create mobile user profile:', createError);
+        // Attempt to see if it failed because email is required (common in some schemas)
+        // For now, we assume schema supports it or we handle it.
+        await supabase.auth.signOut();
+        return 'Failed to create user profile. Please contact support.';
+      }
+      userProfile = newProfile;
+
+      // Log new user
+      await logActivity(
+        'new_user',
+        `New ${userRole} signed up via Mobile: ${userName}`,
+        userId
+      );
+    }
+
+    if (!userProfile) {
+      await supabase.auth.signOut();
+      return 'User profile error.';
+    }
+
+    // Role Specific Redirects
+    // UPDATE: We do NOT redirect anymore. We sign out and let client show success modal.
+
+    const normalizedRole = userProfile.role?.toLowerCase() || '';
+
+    console.log('✅ MOBILE SIGNUP COMPLETE. Signing out to enforce manual login.');
+    await supabase.auth.signOut();
+
+    return {
+      success: true,
+      message: 'Account created successfully. Please log in.',
+      role: normalizedRole
+    };
+  }
 }
